# Commit 013560

Commit hash: 9bbf0bd8bdb2421b158512c750139fd6dff70a26
Commited at: 2024-04-25T05:07:44-07:00
Previous commit hash: 93b860c4d9e4285746eccf64a932d639e3a4e176
Next commit hash: d31ca0d8c91cd63abbfd70887a4dc82d07f6430e
Commit title: 'plain' error format
Commit body:
Summary:
This is the format you get with `--error-format plain`. It's used for test outputs, so many test outputs are changed.

With the introduction of hack warnings, we need to differentiate between errors and warnings in the outputs.

So far, errors with that format looked like:
```
File "function_return_2.bad.php", line 18, characters 10-13:
Invalid return type (Typing[4110])
  File "function_return_2.bad.php", line 17, characters 19-19:
  Expected `G`
  File "function_return_2.bad.php", line 4, characters 16-18:
  But got `int`
```
With this diff, if the error has severity 'error', it will be printed as:
```
ERROR: File "function_return_2.bad.php", line 18, characters 10-13:
Invalid return type (Typing[4110])
  File "function_return_2.bad.php", line 17, characters 19-19:
  Expected `G`
  File "function_return_2.bad.php", line 4, characters 16-18:
  But got `int`
```
If it has severity warning, it will be printed like:
```
WARN: File "function_return_2.bad.php", line 18, characters 10-13:
Invalid return type (Typing[4110])
  File "function_return_2.bad.php", line 17, characters 19-19:
  Expected `G`
  File "function_return_2.bad.php", line 4, characters 16-18:
  But got `int`
```

Reviewed By: francesco-zappa-nardelli

Differential Revision: D56301941
